{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011Cf3Mr3gomETDtqh89XtBv","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_014sb6VmSjKKTe2JU14dZtS2","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/spmv_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_hybrid_r5/spmv/spmv.cpp","content":"// Hybrid MPI + OpenMP + CUDA implementation of the SpMV benchmark.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * MPI   : the matrix rows are partitioned across ranks with a load balance\n//             based on the number of non-zeros per rank. Each rank only stores\n//             (and multiplies) its own row slice; the dense input vector is\n//             replicated. Rank 0 generates the matrix (the RNG sequence of the\n//             original code must be preserved bit-for-bit) and distributes the\n//             slices.\n//   * CUDA  : every rank drives one GPU (round robin over the GPUs visible on\n//             its compute node) and executes the local SpMV with a CSR\n//             \"vector\" kernel using a tunable number of threads per row.\n//   * OpenMP: host side work (reference solution, packing/unpacking of the\n//             distributed slices) is threaded.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                                      \\\n        const cudaError_t status__ = (call);                                                  \\\n        if (status__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                        \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorName(status__),        \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(status__));                        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//   (OpenMP threaded over the rows; rows are independent)\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// CUDA kernel: CSR SpMV, TPR (threads per row) cooperating threads per row.\n// The partial products of one row are reduced within a sub-warp group using\n// warp shuffles; rows are distributed in a grid stride fashion.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int TPR>\n__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                           const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t numRows,\n                           double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (TPR - 1);\n    const unsigned int gid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const unsigned int groupsPerGrid = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / TPR;\n\n    for (index_t row = gid / TPR; row < numRows; row += groupsPerGrid) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += TPR) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = TPR / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, TPR);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Launch helper selecting the number of threads per row at compile time\nstatic void launchSpmv(int threadsPerRow, int blocks, int blockSize, cudaStream_t stream,\n                       const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n                       const double* vec, index_t numRows, double* out) {\n    switch (threadsPerRow) {\n        case 2:\n            spmvKernel<2><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            spmvKernel<4><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvKernel<8><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvKernel<16><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        default:\n            spmvKernel<32><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    bool ok = true;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(&& : ok)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                ok = false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                ok = false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Send/receive potentially huge buffers (element counts may exceed the range of\n// the int used for MPI counts), split into chunks.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstatic void sendChunked(const T* buf, uint64_t count, int dest, int tag, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    constexpr uint64_t kChunk = 1u << 26;  // elements per message\n    uint64_t offset = 0;\n    while (offset < count) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min<uint64_t>(kChunk, count - offset));\n        MPI_Send(buf + offset, n * static_cast<int>(sizeof(T)), MPI_BYTE, dest, tag, comm);\n        offset += static_cast<uint64_t>(n);\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nstatic void recvChunked(T* buf, uint64_t count, int src, int tag, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    constexpr uint64_t kChunk = 1u << 26;\n    uint64_t offset = 0;\n    while (offset < count) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min<uint64_t>(kChunk, count - offset));\n        MPI_Recv(buf + offset, n * static_cast<int>(sizeof(T)), MPI_BYTE, src, tag, comm,\n                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        offset += static_cast<uint64_t>(n);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiThreadLevel = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiThreadLevel);\n\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    int rank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n\n    index_t numRows = 1024;\n    index_t sparsity = 10;\n    index_t iterations = 10;\n    double maxVal = 1.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments (identical on every rank)\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numRows = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n        printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n        printf(\"Non-zero Each rank only stores\n//             (and multiplies) its own row slice; the dense input vector is\n//             replicated. Rank 0 generates the matrix (the RNG sequence of the\n//             original code must be preserved bit-for-bit) and distributes the\n//             slices.\n//   * CUDA  : every rank drives one GPU (round robin over the GPUs visible on\n//             its compute node) and executes the local SpMV with a CSR\n//             \"vector\" kernel using a tunable number of threads per row.\n//   * OpenMP: host side work (reference solution, packing/unpacking of the\n//             distributed slices) is threaded.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                                      \\\n        const cudaError_t status__ = (call);                                                  \\\n        if (status__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                        \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorName(status__),        \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(status__));                        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// threaded over the rows; rows are independent)\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// CUDA kernel: CSR SpMV, TPR (threads per row) cooperating threads per row.\n// The partial products of one row are reduced within a sub-warp group using\n// warp shuffles; rows are distributed in a grid stride fashion.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int TPR>\n__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                           const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t numRows,\n                           double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (TPR - 1);\n    const unsigned int gid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const unsigned int groupsPerGrid = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / TPR;\n\n    for (index_t row = gid / TPR; row < numRows; row += groupsPerGrid) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, TPR);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Launch helper selecting the number of threads per row at compile time\nstatic void launchSpmv(int threadsPerRow, int blocks, int blockSize, cudaStream_t stream,\n                       const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n                       const double* vec, index_t numRows, double* out) {\n    switch (threadsPerRow) {\n        case 2:\n            spmvKernel<2><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            spmvKernel<4><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvKernel<8><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n spmvKernel<32><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n// huge buffers (element counts may exceed the range of\n// the int used for MPI counts), split into chunks.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstatic void sendChunked(const T* buf, uint64_t count, int dest, int tag, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    constexpr uint64_t kChunk = 1u << 26;  // elements per message\n    uint64_t offset = 0;\n    while (offset < count) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min<uint64_t>(kChunk, count - offset));\n        MPI_Send(buf + offset, n * static_cast<int>(sizeof(T)), MPI_BYTE, dest, tag, comm);\n        offset += static_cast<uint64_t>(n);\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nstatic void recvChunked(T* buf, uint64_t count, int src, int tag, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    constexpr uint64_t kChunk = 1u << 26;\n    uint64_t offset = 0;\n    while (offset < count) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min<uint64_t>(kChunk, count - offset));\n        MPI_Recv(buf + offset, n * static_cast<int>(sizeof(T)), MPI_BYTE, src, tag, comm,\n                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        offset += static_cast<uint64_t>(n);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiThreadLevel = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiThreadLevel);\n\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    int rank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, Each rank only stores\n//             (and multiplies) its own row slice; the dense input vector is\n//             replicated. Rank 0 generates the matrix (the RNG sequence of the\n//             original code must be preserved bit-for-bit) and distributes the\n//             slices.\n//   * CUDA  : every rank drives one GPU (round robin over the GPUs visible on\n//             its compute node) and executes the local SpMV with a CSR\n//             \"vector\" kernel using a tunable number of threads per row.\n//   * OpenMP: host side work (reference solution, packing/unpacking of the\n//             distributed slices) is threaded.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                                      \\\n        const cudaError_t status__ = (call);                                                  \\\n        if (status__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                        \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorName(status__),        \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(status__));                        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//   (OpenMP threaded over the rows; rows are independent)\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// CUDA kernel: CSR SpMV, TPR (threads per row) cooperating threads per row.\n// The partial products of one row are reduced within a sub-warp group using\n// warp shuffles; rows are distributed in a grid stride fashion.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int TPR>\n__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                           const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t numRows,\n                           double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (TPR - 1);\n    const unsigned int gid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const unsigned int groupsPerGrid = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / TPR;\n\n    for (index_t row = gid / TPR; row < numRows; row += groupsPerGrid) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += TPR) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = TPR / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, TPR);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Launch helper selecting the number of threads per row at compile time\nstatic void launchSpmv(int threadsPerRow, int blocks, int blockSize, cudaStream_t stream,\n                       const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n                       const double* vec, index_t numRows, double* out) {\n    switch (threadsPerRow) {\n        case 2:\n            spmvKernel<2><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            spmvKernel<4><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvKernel<8><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvKernel<16><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        default:\n            spmvKernel<32><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    bool ok = true;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(&& : ok)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                ok = false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                ok = false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default:       Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Send/receive potentially huge buffers (element counts may exceed the range of\n// the int used for MPI counts), split into chunks.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstatic void sendChunked(const T* buf, uint64_t count, int dest, int tag, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    constexpr uint64_t kChunk = 1u << 26;  // elements per message\n    uint64_t offset = 0;\n    while (offset < count) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min<uint64_t>(kChunk, count - offset));\n        MPI_Send(buf + offset, n * static_cast<int>(sizeof(T)), MPI_BYTE, dest, tag, comm);\n        offset += static_cast<uint64_t>(n);\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nstatic void recvChunked(T* buf, uint64_t count, int src, int tag, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    constexpr uint64_t kChunk = 1u << 26;\n    uint64_t offset = 0;\n    while (offset < count) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min<uint64_t>(kChunk, count - offset));\n        MPI_Recv(buf + offset, n * static_cast<int>(sizeof(T)), MPI_BYTE, src, tag, comm,\n                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        offset += static_cast<uint64_t>(n);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiThreadLevel = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiThreadLevel);\n\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    int rank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD,           if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Calculate number of non-zero elements\n    const index_t nItems = (numRows * numRows) / sparsity;\n\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        printf(\"Sparse Matrix-Vector Multiplication (SpMV) Benchmark\\n\");\n        printf(\"Matrix size: %u x %u\\n\", numRows, numRows);\n        printf(\"Sparsity: 1 out of %u entries is non-zero\\n\", sparsity);\n        printf(\"Non-zero     fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on rank %d\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaSetDevice(device));\n    CUDA_CHECK(cudaFree(nullptr));  // establish the context before timing\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Data std::vector<index_t> h_rowDelimiters;\n    std::vector<double> h_vec(numRows);  // dense vector, replicated on all ranks\n    std::vector<double> h_out;           // full output vector (rank 0)\n\n printf(\"Initializing data structures...\\n\");\n        fill(h_vec.data(), numRows, maxVal);\n        fill(h_val.data(), nItems, maxVal);\n        initRandomMatrix(h_cols.data(), h_rowDelimiters.data(), nItems, numRows);\n    }\n\n    MPI_Bcast(h_vec.data(), static_cast<int>(numRows), MPI_DOUBLE, 0, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    // Row partitioning, balanced by number of non-zeros\n    // ------------------------------------------------------------------\n    std::vector<index_t> rowStart(static_cast<size_t>(numRanks) + 1, 0);\n    if (rank == 0) {\n        for (int r = 0; r <= numRanks; ++r) {\n            const uint64_t target = (static_cast<uint64_t>(nItems) * r) / numRanks;\n            // First row whose starting offset is >= target\n            index_t lo = 0, hi = numRows;\n            while (lo < hi) {\n                const index_t mid = lo + (hi - lo) / 2;\n                if (h_rowDelimiters[mid] < target) {\n                    lo = mid + 1;\n                } else {\n                    hi = mid;\n                }\n            }\n            rowStart[r] = lo;\n        }\n        rowStart[0] = 0;\n        rowStart[numRanks] = numRows;\n        // keep the partition monotone\n        for (int r = 1; r <= numRanks; ++r) {\n            if (rowStart[r] < rowStart[r - 1]) rowStart[r] = rowStart[r - 1];\n        }\n    }\n    MPI_Bcast(rowStart.data(), Each rank only stores\n//             (and multiplies) its own row slice; the dense input vector is\n//             replicated. Rank 0 generates the matrix (the RNG sequence of the\n//             original code must be preserved bit-for-bit) and distributes the\n//             slices.\n//   * CUDA  : every rank drives one GPU (round robin over the GPUs visible on\n//             its compute node) and executes the local SpMV with a CSR\n//             \"vector\" kernel using a tunable number of threads per row.\n//   * OpenMP: host side work (reference solution, packing/unpacking of the\n//             distributed slices) is threaded.\n\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <cuda_runtime.h>\n#include <mpi.h>\n#include <omp.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nusing index_t = uint32_t;\n\n// Constants\nconstexpr double MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR = 0.02;\n\n#define CUDA_CHECK(call)                                                                      \\\n    do {                                                                                      \\\n        const cudaError_t status__ = (call);                                                  \\\n        if (status__ != cudaSuccess) {                                                        \\\n            fprintf(stderr, \"CUDA error %s at %s:%d: %s\\n\", cudaGetErrorName(status__),        \\\n                    __FILE__, __LINE__, cudaGetErrorString(status__));                        \\\n            MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);                                                     \\\n        }                                                                                     \\\n    } while (0)\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: fill\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Initialize array with random values\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   A: pointer to the array to initialize\n//   n: number of elements in the array\n//   maxVal: specifies range of random values [0, maxVal]\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid fill(double* A, const index_t n, const double maxVal) {\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n        A[i] = maxVal * (rand() / (static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX) + 1.0));\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: initRandomMatrix\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Assigns random positions to a given number of elements in a square\n//   matrix. The function encodes these positions in compressed sparse\n//   row (CSR) format.\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   cols:          array for column indexes of elements (size should be = n)\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   n:             number of nonzero elements\n//   dim:           number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid initRandomMatrix(index_t* cols, index_t* rowDelimiters, const index_t n, const index_t dim) {\n    index_t nnzAssigned = 0;\n\n    // Figure out the probability that a nonzero should be assigned\n    double prob = static_cast<double>(n) / (static_cast<double>(dim) * static_cast<double>(dim));\n\n    // Seed random number generator\n    srand(8675309);\n\n    // Randomly decide whether entry i,j gets a value, but ensure n values are assigned\n    bool fillRemaining = false;\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        rowDelimiters[i] = nnzAssigned;\n        for (index_t j = 0; j < dim; ++j) {\n            index_t numEntriesLeft = (dim * dim) - ((i * dim) + j);\n            index_t needToAssign = n - nnzAssigned;\n            if (numEntriesLeft <= needToAssign) {\n                fillRemaining = true;\n            }\n            double randVal = static_cast<double>(rand()) / RAND_MAX;\n            if ((nnzAssigned < n && randVal <= prob) || fillRemaining) {\n                // Assign (i,j) a value\n                cols[nnzAssigned] = j;\n                nnzAssigned++;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    // Convention: put the number of non-zeroes at the end of the row delimiters array\n    rowDelimiters[dim] = n;\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: spmvCpu\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Computes sparse matrix-vector multiplication using CSR format\n//   (OpenMP threaded over the rows; rows are independent)\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   val: array holding the non-zero values for the matrix\n//   cols: array of column indices for each element\n//   rowDelimiters: array of size dim+1 holding indices to rows;\n//                  last element is the index one past the last element\n//   vec: dense vector of size dim to be used for multiplication\n//   dim: number of rows/columns in the matrix\n//   out: output - result from the spmv calculation\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nvoid spmvCpu(const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n             const double* vec, const index_t dim, double* out) {\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < dim; ++i) {\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowDelimiters[i]; j < rowDelimiters[i + 1]; ++j) {\n            const auto col = cols[j];\n            t += val[j] * vec[col];\n        }\n        out[i] = t;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// CUDA kernel: CSR SpMV, TPR (threads per row) cooperating threads per row.\n// The partial products of one row are reduced within a sub-warp group using\n// warp shuffles; rows are distributed in a grid stride fashion.\n// ****************************************************************************\ntemplate <int TPR>\n__global__ void spmvKernel(const double* __restrict__ val, const index_t* __restrict__ cols,\n                           const index_t* __restrict__ rowDelimiters,\n                           const double* __restrict__ vec, const index_t numRows,\n                           double* __restrict__ out) {\n    const unsigned int lane = threadIdx.x & (TPR - 1);\n    const unsigned int gid = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const unsigned int groupsPerGrid = (gridDim.x * blockDim.x) / TPR;\n\n    for (index_t row = gid / TPR; row < numRows; row += groupsPerGrid) {\n        const index_t rowStart = rowDelimiters[row];\n        const index_t rowEnd = rowDelimiters[row + 1];\n\n        double t = 0.0;\n        for (index_t j = rowStart + lane; j < rowEnd; j += TPR) {\n            t += val[j] * __ldg(&vec[cols[j]]);\n        }\n\n#pragma unroll\n        for (int offset = TPR / 2; offset > 0; offset >>= 1) {\n            t += __shfl_down_sync(0xffffffffu, t, offset, TPR);\n        }\n\n        if (lane == 0) {\n            out[row] = t;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Launch helper selecting the number of threads per row at compile time\nstatic void launchSpmv(int threadsPerRow, int blocks, int blockSize, cudaStream_t stream,\n                       const double* val, const index_t* cols, const index_t* rowDelimiters,\n                       const double* vec, index_t numRows, double* out) {\n    switch (threadsPerRow) {\n        case 2:\n            spmvKernel<2><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 4:\n            spmvKernel<4><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 8:\n            spmvKernel<8><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        case 16:\n            spmvKernel<16><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n        default:\n            spmvKernel<32><<<blocks, blockSize, 0, stream>>>(val, cols, rowDelimiters, vec, numRows, out);\n            break;\n    }\n}\n\n// ****************************************************************************\n// Function: verifyResults\n//\n// Purpose:\n//   Verifies correctness of results by comparing to reference solution\n//\n// Arguments:\n//   reference: array holding the reference result vector\n//   result: array holding the result vector to verify\n//   size: number of elements per vector\n//\n// ****************************************************************************\nbool verifyResults(const double* reference, const double* result, const index_t size) {\n    bool ok = true;\n#pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) reduction(&& : ok)\n    for (index_t i = 0; i < size; ++i) {\n        const double ref = reference[i];\n        const double res = result[i];\n        if (std::abs(ref) < 1e-10) {\n            // For very small values, check absolute error\n            if (std::abs(res) > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e\\n\", i, ref, res);\n                ok = false;\n            }\n        } else {\n            // Check relative error\n            const double relError = std::abs((res - ref) / ref);\n            if (relError > MAX_RELATIVE_ERROR) {\n                printf(\"Validation failed at index %u: reference %.10e, got %.10e (rel error: %.10e)\\n\",\n                       i, ref, res, relError);\n                ok = false;\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    return ok;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of rows/columns in the matrix (default: 1024)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -s <num>     Sparsity: 1 out of N entries is non-zero (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -m <val>     Maximum value for matrix and vector elements (default: 1.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\n// Send/receive potentially huge buffers (element counts may exceed the range of\n// the int used for MPI counts), split into chunks.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstatic void sendChunked(const T* buf, uint64_t count, int dest, int tag, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    constexpr uint64_t kChunk = 1u << 26;  // elements per message\n    uint64_t offset = 0;\n    while (offset < count) {\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(std::min<uint64_t>(kChunk, count - offset));\n        MPI_Send(buf + offset, n * static_cast<int>(sizeof(T)), MPI_BYTE, dest, tag, comm);\n        offset += static_cast<uint64_t>(n);\n    }\n}\n\ntemplate <typename T>\nstatic void recvChunked(T* buf, uint64_t count, int src, int tag, MPI_Comm comm) {\n    constexpr uint64_t kChunk = 1u << 26;\n MPI_BYTE, src, tag, comm,\n                 MPI_STATUS_IGNORE);\n        offset += static_cast<uint64_t>(n);\n    }\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int mpiThreadLevel = 0;\n    MPI_Init_thread(&argc, &argv, MPI_THREAD_FUNNELED, &mpiThreadLevel);\n\n    int numRanks = 1;\n    int rank = 0;\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &numRanks);\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            sparsity = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            iterations = static_cast<index_t>(atoi(argv[++i]));\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-m\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            maxVal = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n     fprintf(stderr, \"No CUDA device available on rank %d\\n\", rank);\n        MPI_Abort(MPI_COMM_WORLD, 1);\n    }\n    const int device = localRank % deviceCount;\n The local slice lives entirely on the GPU; the kernel is a CSR \"vector\" kernel with a compile-time threads-per-row group (2/4/8/16/32, chosen from the average row length), warp-shuffle reduction per row, `__ldg` on the vector, and grid-stride row assignment with the grid sized to the SM count.
